CStrings:
+ " apps and websites"
+ " to restrict access to Siri AI and to not allow Explicit Language. Turning on Accessibility Ask may allow access to Apple Intelligence Q&A capabilities and explicit language in responses in places like Magnifier and VoiceOver."
+ " to restrict access to Siri AI and to not allow Math Assistance tools or Explicit Language. Turning on Accessibility Ask may allow access to Apple Intelligence Q&A capabilities, math help, and explicit language in responses in places like Magnifier and VoiceOver."
+ " to restrict access to Siri AI and to not allow Math Assistance tools. Turning on Accessibility Ask may allow access to Apple Intelligence Q&A capabilities and math help in places like Magnifier and VoiceOver."
+ " to restrict access to Siri AI and to not allow Writing Assistance or Math Assistance tools. Turning on Accessibility Ask may allow access to Apple Intelligence Q&A capabilities, writing help, and math help in places like Magnifier and VoiceOver."
+ " to restrict access to Siri AI and to not allow Writing Assistance tools or Explicit Language. Turning on Accessibility Ask may allow access to Apple Intelligence Q&A capabilities, writing help, and explicit language in responses in places like Magnifier and VoiceOver."
+ " to restrict access to Siri AI and to not allow Writing Assistance tools, Math Assistance tools, or Explicit Language. Turning on Accessibility Ask may allow access to Apple Intelligence Q&A capabilities, writing help, math help, and explicit language in responses in places like Magnifier and VoiceOver."
+ " to restrict access to Siri AI and to not allow Writing Assistance tools. Turning on Accessibility Ask may allow access to Apple Intelligence Q&A capabilities and writing help in places like Magnifier and VoiceOver."
+ " to restrict access to Siri AI. Turning on Accessibility Ask may allow access to Apple Intelligence Q&A capabilities in places like Magnifier and VoiceOver."

+ ". Turning on Accessibility Ask may allow explicit language in responses in places like Magnifier and VoiceOver."
+ ". Turning on Accessibility Ask may allow math help and explicit language in responses in places like Magnifier and VoiceOver."
+ ". Turning on Accessibility Ask may allow math help in places like Magnifier and VoiceOver."
+ ". Turning on Accessibility Ask may allow writing and math help in places like Magnifier and VoiceOver."
+ ". Turning on Accessibility Ask may allow writing help and explicit language in responses in places like Magnifier and VoiceOver."
+ ". Turning on Accessibility Ask may allow writing help in places like Magnifier and VoiceOver."
+ ". Turning on Accessibility Ask may allow writing help, math help, and explicit language in responses in places like Magnifier and VoiceOver."
+ "Alert shown when a Screen Time change is attempted on a store demo device."
+ "Allow Accessibility Ask?"
+ "Allows Siri to search the web to answer general knowledge questions. When blocked, Siri can still help with tasks, basic math, and real-time information like weather and sports."
+ "Allows extensions to provide additional text and image capabilities, including ChatGPT. Extensions with their own app can also be limited in Time Allowances."
+ "Allows extensions to provide additional text and image capabilities, including ChatGPT. Extensions with their own app can also be limited in Time Allowances. Not available for users under 13."
+ "Apps and websites in this category will be moved to their closest matching default category (Entertainment, Games, or Social Media), or to All Others if no match exists."
+ "Apps and websites in this category will be moved to their closest matching default category (Entertainment, Games, or Social Media), or to All Others if no match exists. This time allowance category will be removed from all of ^["
+ "Apps and websites in this category will be moved to their closest matching default category (Entertainment, Games, or Social Media), or to All Others if no match exists. This time allowance category will be removed from all of your child’s devices."
+ "Apps and websites in this category will be moved to their closest matching default category (Entertainment, Games, or Social Media), or to All Others if no match exists. This time allowance category will be removed from all of your devices."
+ "Ask About Images and Surroundings"
+ "Based on the study from most pediatric sleep experts, 10-13 hours of sleep is recommended for a 10-year-old."
+ "Blocking Screen Time change; device is in Store Demo Mode."
+ "Button that confirms turning off Share Across Devices."
+ "Button that confirms turning on Share Across Devices."
+ "Controls whether Accessibility features can use Apple Intelligence to answer questions about images on-device and in your surroundings using features such as Ask Magnifier, Image Explorer, and Ask in Live Recognition."

+ "Get insights about your screen time and set limits as needed. Adults can also set parental controls for a child’s device."
